278 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [OCT.-DEC., (Received, 17th January 1906.; CUSTOM HOUSE, KIAOCHOW, ioth January 1906. SIR, I.&mdash;a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the fourth quarter of the year 1905. b. The Custom House (jff- HU) was opened on the ist July 1899. The collection of Tariff Import and Export Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.Tfs. 30 + 80 per picul), and the collection of Native Duties and Likin on Junk Cargoes at half Tariff rates, commenced on that day. The stations at present provisionally controlled by the Kiaochow Customs are known as Tsangkou (fjg&quot; 0); Nuku ( ■k **&amp;gt;; Shatszkou ( including Tengyao (S ; Hungshihyai (ifl -&pound;) JiU ; Taputou i$. 9j|) ; and Lingshanwei (fH |ij Hj), including Tachiangkou c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China, the statistics of goods- landed and consumed in German territory not being included.
